### Register with the Leergeld Foundation
**Organization:** Learning Fund Alblasserwaard Vijfheerenlanden
**URL:** https://diensten.nl/organisation/leergeld-alblasserwaard- Vijfheerenlanden/aanmelden-bij-stichting-leergeld.html

**Objective:** After registering, every new family will have an intake interview with a Leergeld staff member. This interview will help them understand how Leergeld can support you. They will also take the time to explain what you can expect from Leergeld and what is needed to determine your eligibility.

- E-mail: info@leergeldav.nl
- Website: https://leergeldav.nl/

### Sign up for HeusdenPas / In test
**Organization:** Bijeen Heusden
**URL:** https://diensten.nl/organisation/bijeen-heusden/aanmelden-heusdenpas.html

**Objective:** This facility is currently in TEST mode and will go live on October 1st. The free HeusdenPas is available to residents who have to survive on a low income. With the HeusdenPas, you receive a budget that you can use for sports or cultural activities, the library, or the internet. You can also receive a discount on activities in the municipality of Heusden.

**Mission:** Bijeen is a network of organizations active in the municipality of Heusden in the areas of healthcare, youth, employment, and well-being. Together, they ensure that everyone receives the care and support they need.

**Contact information:**
- Telephone: 073-782 0178
- Email: info@bijeenheusden.nl
- Website: https://www.bijeenheusden.nl

---

### Request a financial contribution
**Organization:** Social Fund Leidschendam-Voorburg

School package via the Tubbergen Dinkelland Welfare Foundation (SWTD)
**Organization:** Leergeld Dinkelland
**URL:** https://diensten.nl/organisation/leergeld-dinkelland/schoolpakket-via-stichting-welzijn-tubbergen-dinkelland-swtd.html

**Purpose:** The school package. You can apply for a contribution if your income is less than or equal to 120% of the social minimum for everything your children need for school. The school package is available for children aged 4 to 12 living in the municipality of Dinkelland.

**Mission:** The Leergeld Foundation tries to prevent children living in poverty from becoming socially isolated.
